      "alias": "H10",

      "sheet": "sheet_0001.png",
      "notes": "Happy Millefeuille commu depicts classroom planning and a cafeteria/kitchen peer exchange before an outdoor evening scene. At 88.2 s Hiro asks whether Producer wants her home cooking; at 123.466667 s Producer warns about stumbling in normal classes. China and Ume visibly participate in cooking help, with Ume hands on hips at 264.533333 s; a teal transition occupies F000010. F000011 presents the stomach-grabbing metaphor in Hiro's caption and F000012 Producer's question whether she can make it. This is specific social/work texture, not proof that sweetness or cooking erases bodily difficulty.",

    {
      "alias": "H09",
      "run": "survey",
      "sheet": "sheet_0001.png",
      "notes": "Garakuta Road survey shows classroom recollection, a warmly lit vignette/parenthesized internal-memory layer, then Producer conversation and lesson room. At 63.61355 s Hiro calls something her own memory not shared with China/Ume; at 190.807283 s parenthesized text says she told a tremendous lie and might have told the friends about it. At 360.41005 s she says she worked hard and wants time being appreciated; at 445.211433 s she states Producer acts because he likes it; at 487.603783 s she says he made her hobby brighter. Distinct visible narrative layers and selective disclosure prevent flattening all text into simultaneous voiced confession.",

    {
      "alias": "H11",
      "run": "survey",
      "sheet": "sheet_0001.png",
      "notes": "Costume commu begins at a table, then changes to classroom. Hiro's tilted head, small smiles, hand-at-chest and folded-arm poses coexist with Producer warnings and her claim of yielding to his overprotectiveness for now at 164.9 s. The 311.433333 s sample is an extreme tilted close face with an open mouth and the visible exclamation 'ばぁ！'; this is an overtly staged surprise, a visual/textual counterexample to describing all comedy as sincere underreaction. At 421.366667 s Hiro says she wants those inconvenienced by pranks to think today was fun. Audio attack/timing and whether the prank actually succeeded are not established.",

      "alias": "H12",

      "sheet": "sheet_0001.png",
      "notes": "Koukei rendered performance alternates rear views toward a glow-stick audience, closer face/microphone shots, and wide architectural stage views. Arched window forms, luminous blue/green backdrop imagery, low frontal audience silhouettes and bright beams surround a pale costume. Samples include a raised arm near the head, extended arm, downward glance and a final rear silhouette beneath a flare. F000012 is black.       "notes": "The improvement fan edit presents training-room tracksuit shots, outdoor/campus stage contexts, then the elaborate pale-green costume and arched-window concert set.       "alias": "H14",

      "sheet": "sheet_0001.png",
      "notes": "Authored Koukei is drawn/graphic rather than a recording of the H12 rendered stage. Survey includes pastel geometric fields, vines, simplified reaching Hiro, objects/food and camera-like devices around her, then darker grainy outlined portraits crossed by bright diagonal streaks. An early foundational work already includes cute/playful object collage alongside optical fragmentation; visual cuteness cannot be reserved for a later normalized phase.       "notes": "Contrast rendered performance centers a circular illuminated aperture and dark asymmetric costume. Samples alternate open palms, diagonally cropped close face, high/wide stage views, a hand masking one eye in stark side lighting, and the rear audience view.     {
      "alias": "H17",
      "run": "survey",
      "sheet": "sheet_0001.png",
      "notes": "Sunfaded rendered stage combines large rectangular frame/door-like elements, arched architecture, bright woodland/sky imagery, audience glow sticks and mixed warm/cool light. Samples show arms spread near a frame, profile toward illuminated foliage, a raised hand and a close hand-shaded brow, ending on a smiling face. This shares some stage vocabulary with Koukei while giving the frame/threshold and broad open-arm pose particular salience; it does not establish that later development caused more bodily fluency.",

    {
      "alias": "H18",
      "run": "survey",
      "sheet": "sheet_0001.png",
      "notes": "The official Sunfaded MV is visibly authored and changing: low-angle illustrated Hiro in an urban environment; utility wires and sky; a silhouetted hand before sunlight; kaleidoscopic mirrored Hiro; dice; blurred portrait/text fragments; overhead crosswalk; a manga-like shoe panel; credits; and a later pale costume/object collage. The source moves among character, objects, city space, optical multiplication and printed graphic forms. It cannot be collapsed into H17's literal concert stage or called a static recording cover, and these visuals alone do not establish its harmony, rhythm or acoustic experimentalism.",

      "alias": "H21",

      "notes": "Howling over the World uses an urban/digital setting with monitor imagery, chains/fences, luminous repeated bodies or reflections, fragmented/pixelated portraits, high/low angles and red/blue/green color fields.       "alias": "H27",
      "run": "survey",
      "sheet": "sheet_0001.png",
      "notes": "Garakuta Road rendered survey uses a large multilevel star/screen concert set, glow-stick crowd, colored spotlights and a dark jacket with bright sleeve trim. Close smiling gesture shots coexist with extreme wide stage framing and a boot detail. The final sample has a closed-eye smile and two-finger facial gesture. This supplies a public playful/presentational counterpart to the private commu's cute desire, but does not prove that every gesture literalizes that confession or that it occupies the same story instant.",

    {
      "alias": "H28",
      "run": "survey",
      "sheet": "sheet_0001.png",
      "notes": "Michinaru Hirogaru rendered duet repeatedly places Hiro and China together on a colorful circular stage. Rear two-body orientation, Hiro leaning forward beside China, facing/offered hands, different-height poses and side-by-side turns all appear in the sample set. Both also receive locally differentiated framing rather than one remaining generic scenery. This expands the observed local reciprocity beyond the older single reaching anchor, while still not measuring whole-song screen time, vocal distribution or equitable relationship authorship.",

      "alias": "H31",
      "run": "survey",
      "sheet": "sheet_0001.png",
      "notes": "The Halloween/carnival staging places Hiro in a small hat and a multicolored orange/black costume among pumpkins, a toy bear, rope, neon arrows and bunting. Sampled poses include open arms, hands extended, rear audience-facing stance and raised arms; an overhead view shows a star-shaped platform. A toy-bear close-up interrupts the body views. Theatrical costume play and prop inserts are visible; they do not turn a near-zero/zero-P10 audio ratio into expressive vocal range. Opening UI and final black are retained as context.",

      "notes": "The number-one declaration is followed at 2923.003417 by Hiro visibly leaning beside Sena with spiral eyes and the caption apologizing that her limit is near. The next sampled frames show stairs and then the wide stage. This directly juxtaposes victory with represented bodily limitation, without measuring physiology or sustained stability. At 3246–3252 Hiro requests a new hearing of her dream and says she will become a top idol; at 3255 she says this time properly. The Producer's displayed question at 3258 asks whether they may again share the same dream. Hiro's expression changes to a smile at 3261; a white transition and illustrated promise at 3264–3267 finish the sequence. A renewed declared project is visible; a demonstrated non-crisis professional floor is not.",

      "notes": "The 143–157 Contrast interval alternates wide audience, full body, rear audience-facing and close facial views around a luminous circular flower display. Hands are raised near the face, opened outward and lowered toward the chest; visible lyrics at 151–153 ask to be looked at, with an invitation to join at 155–157. This supports a local staging of address and shared attention. The words are a song persona and the points are poses: neither gives a direct measure of vocal projection, phrase continuity or narrative chronology.",

      "notes": "Sunfaded's 63–77 points set a raised hand, frontal address and extended arm against a freestanding rectangular frame, broad audience fields and a wooded-light backdrop. The visual field changes from cool architecture to warm foliage. This can support a comparison of framing and address with Koukei/Contrast, while shared arched-stage and arm-opening devices prevent treating it as a unique proof of bodily emancipation. Captions about voice are visible lyrics, not a newly heard vocal quality.",

      "notes": "The 51–65 Halloween sequence includes a wide audience-side view, hands opened toward the camera at 53, very wide stage, a red toy-bear insert at 57, a raised-knee pose at 59, lateral body views at 61–63 and overhead star-floor composition at 65. The prop interruption and dramatic change of visual scale complicate any claim that a visual-difference proxy measures Hiro's own movement.       "notes": "At 101–103 dense repeated Japanese text and scribbled lines fill the frame with RGB offsets; 105 is a mostly dark interface-like field. At 107–115 a repeated sideways illustrated portrait returns with changing crop, type, overlays and glitch-like line graphics.
